We empirically verify that entropy jumps are a useful proxy for decision points and, in a stylized model of reasoning, prove that our method's mixing time scales with the number of decisions in a trace rather than with the number of tokens, which can be much larger. Across MATH500, HumanEval, GPQA Diamond, and AIME26, our method consistently improves over baselines and RL-trained models.

</details>


### [182] [SoundnessBench: Can Your AI Scientist Really Tell Good Research Ideas from Bad Ones?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30329)
*Sy-Tuyen Ho,Minghui Liu,Huy Nghiem,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SoundnessBench基准，用于评估大语言模型判断机器学习研究提案方法论可靠性的能力。测试发现现有模型普遍存在乐观偏见，无法可靠地作为科学严谨性的独立初审评估者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准很少测试一个基本瓶颈：大语言模型能否在投入时间和计算资源之前判断研究想法的方法论可行性。自主AI研究代理旨在加速科学发现，但缺乏对这一关键能力的评估。

Method: 构建了包含1,099个机器学习研究提案的SoundnessBench基准，这些提案从ICLR投稿重建，标注了审稿人可靠性子分数，并对照源论文进行了审计。在12个前沿大语言模型上进行测试，并控制了公共语料库污染、论文识别短语、表面特征和人工审核质量等混淆因素。

Result: 在标准提示下，模型普遍存在乐观偏见，经常将低可靠性提案评为可靠；激进的提示策略主要将错误从假阳性转移到假阴性。控制实验表明这种行为不能由单一混淆因素解释。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型尚未达到可以作为科学严谨性独立初审评估者的可靠性水平，需要进一步改进才能在实际研究流程中应用。

Abstract: Autonomous AI research agents aim to accelerate scientific discovery by automating the research pipeline, from hypothesis generation to peer review. However, existing benchmarks rarely test a fundamental bottleneck: whether Large Language Models can judge the methodological viability of a research idea before expending time and computational resources. We introduce SoundnessBench, a curated benchmark of 1,099 machine-learning research proposals reconstructed from ICLR submissions, labeled with reviewer soundness sub-scores, and audited against source papers. SoundnessBench should be interpreted as a benchmark for recoverable proposal-stage soundness rather than exact prediction of full-paper review outcomes. Across 12 frontier LLMs, we find a pervasive optimism bias: under standard prompting, models frequently rate low-soundness proposals as sound, while aggressive prompting largely shifts errors from false positives to false negatives. Additional controls for public-corpus contamination, paper-identifying phrases, surface features, and human audit quality suggest that this behavior is not explained by a single confounder. Our results indicate that current LLMs are not yet reliable as standalone first-gate evaluators for scientific rigor.

</details>


### [183] [When, why, and how do diffusion posterior samplers fail? A finite-sample lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30330)
*Benjamin A. Burns,Sara Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从有限样本视角分析了扩散模型在成像逆问题后验采样中的似然近似误差，揭示了这些近似会导致后验分布的误估、模式权重不准确和幻觉现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型方法在后验采样中必须使用不精确的似然近似以保持计算可行性，虽然这些近似在经验上表现良好，但其对采样后验的下游影响尚不明确，可能导致无法解释的失败案例。

Method: 引入有限样本后验采样视角，该视角随着训练集规模趋于无穷大可将后验近似到任意精度，适用于任何前向模型和先验分布；使用该框架分析流行后验采样近似方法的误差传播机制。

Result: 发现流行后验采样近似倾向于在中间时间步低估或高估后验扩散，导致对早期停止时间敏感、后验模式相对权重不准确，以及幻觉现象（先验模式幻觉和似然模式幻觉）；这些误差可在仅有非线性的测量模型或多模态后验的情况下，仅因多模态先验和中间采样时间的不准确后验扩散而产生。

Conclusion: 提出的有限样本后验采样方法与似然近似类型和前向模型类型无关，可作为即插即用的诊断工具来评估现有和未来后验采样器的准确性和失败模式。

Abstract: Diffusion models have excellent capacity to model complex distributions of natural data, which has made them a popular and effective choice for posterior sampling in imaging inverse problems. Existing methods can incorporate any measurement model at inference time but must use an inexact approximation for the likelihood at intermediate timesteps for computational tractability. Although these approximations can often work well empirically, their downstream effect on the sampled posterior is poorly understood and can result in unexplained failures. To understand when, why, and how these likelihood approximations propagate to erroneous posterior distributions, we introduce a finite-sample perspective on posterior sampling that approximates the posterior to arbitrary precision as training set size tends towards infinity, for any forward model and prior distribution. Using this finite-sample lens, we observe that popular posterior sampling approximations tend to under- or over-estimate the spread of the posterior at intermediate timesteps, causing downstream consequences including sensitivity to early stopping time, inaccurate relative weighting of posterior modes, and hallucination, both of prior modes that are not in the posterior and likelihood modes that are not supported by the prior. Moreover, we find that the cause of these posterior errors requires neither a nonlinear measurement model nor a multimodal posterior, but can arise solely due to a multimodal prior and inaccurate posterior spread at intermediate sampling times. Our finite-sample posterior sampling approach is agnostic to the type of likelihood approximation and the type of (linear or nonlinear) forward model, and can thus serve as a drop-in diagnostic to evaluate the accuracy and failure modes of existing and future posterior samplers.

</details>


### [184] [Fairness-Aware Federated Learning with Trajectory Shapley Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30336)
*Daniel Kuznetsov,Ziqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了轨迹沙普利值（TSV），一种基于优化轨迹的联邦学习贡献评估指标，并据此设计了自适应聚合方法FedTSV，实现了更公平、鲁棒的联邦优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习聚合方案使用固定权重，无法反映客户端贡献的不平等性和时变性，导致学习过程存在偏差和不稳定性。现有方法缺乏对异质性和对抗性参与的有效应对机制。

Method: 提出轨迹沙普利值（TSV），通过基于验证集的时序一致性效用函数评估每个客户端对全局模型优化轨迹的影响；基于TSV设计FedTSV自适应聚合方法，将每轮评估转换为动态客户端权重，使服务器能够实时响应异质性和对抗性参与。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，FedTSV加速了模型收敛，提升了鲁棒性，并产生更公平的贡献评估结果，为公平感知的联邦优化提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: FedTSV方法成功解决了联邦学习中客户端贡献评估的公平性问题，通过动态权重机制实现了更稳定和鲁棒的学习过程，为公平感知联邦优化奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Federated learning is an emerging distributed paradigm that addresses the challenges posed by heterogeneous, privacy-sensitive data. It enables multiple clients to train a model collaboratively by aggregating their local updates at a server. However, conventional aggregation schemes typically use fixed weights that fail to reflect unequal and time-varying client contributions, leading to biased and unstable learning. To improve fairness and stability, we propose the Trajectory Shapley Value (TSV), a contribution metric that evaluates how each client influences the optimization trajectory of the global model using a validation-based, temporally consistent utility. Building on TSV, we design FedTSV, an adaptive aggregation method that converts per-round evaluations into dynamic client weights, allowing the server to respond to heterogeneous and adversarial participation in real time. Experiments on benchmark datasets show that FedTSV accelerates convergence, improves robustness, and yields more equitable contribution assessments, thereby providing a principled foundation for fairness-aware federated optimization.

</details>


### [185] [Efficient Test-Time Finetuning of LLMs via Convex Reconstruction and Gradient Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30337)
*Alaa Khamis,Alaa Maalouf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HullFT是一种几何方法的测试时微调(TTFT)技术，通过Frank-Wolfe优化将查询嵌入表示为训练序列的稀疏凸组合，并使用梯度重用技术降低计算开销，在质量和效率上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时微调(TTFT)需要为每个查询执行检索和微调，速度成为关键瓶颈。现有方法存在质量-速度权衡问题：快速检索往往冗余，而多样性感知的强选择方法则带来过高的每查询成本。

Method: HullFT采用几何方法：(1)使用高效的无需投影的Frank-Wolfe优化，将查询嵌入表示为少量训练序列的稀疏凸组合，获得既相关又多样化的支持集；(2)通过几何整数化过程将分数凸权重转换为精确整数多重集用于微调；(3)利用自然产生的重复样本，采用梯度重用技术分摊重复微调步骤中的前向-反向计算。

Result: 实验结果表明，HullFT在质量-效率权衡上优于当前最先进的TTFT方法，在显著降低总运行时间的同时实现了更低的比特每字节(bits-per-byte)。

Conclusion: HullFT成功解决了TTFT的两个主要瓶颈问题（选择和微调速度），通过几何优化和梯度重用技术实现了质量和效率的双重提升，为测试时微调方法提供了新的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Test-time finetuning (TTFT) is a rapidly evolving paradigm that adapts a language model to each prompt by retrieving related sequences, updating the model on them, and then evaluating the prompt. However, TTFT is only practical if it is fast: selection and finetuning both happen per query, making each a direct bottleneck. Existing methods trade speed for quality: fast retrieval is often redundant, while stronger diversity-aware selection adds prohibitive per-query cost. We introduce HullFT, a geometric approach to TTFT that addresses both bottlenecks. Given a query, HullFT first represents the query embedding as a sparse convex combination of few training sequences, using efficient projection-free Frank-Wolfe optimization. This yields a support set that is inherently relevant and diverse. We then convert the fractional convex weights into an exact integer multiset for finetuning through a geometric integerization procedure. The resulting multiplicities naturally create repeated examples, which we exploit with Gradient Reuse to amortize forward-backward computation across repeated finetuning steps. Our experiments show that HullFT improves the quality-efficiency tradeoff over current state-of-the-art TTFT methods, achieving lower bits-per-byte at substantially lower total runtime.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [HPC-vQPU: A Service-Export Architecture for Virtual QPUs on Batch-Scheduled HPC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28845)
*Shusen Liu,Pascal Jahan Elahi,Ugo Varetto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了HPC-vQPU架构，在批处理调度的HPC系统上安全地导出设备保真的虚拟量子处理单元服务，解决了安全超算环境与量子软件交互接口之间的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备感知量子仿真日益需要HPC级加速器，但安全超算环境提供的是批处理调度执行环境，而非量子软件期望的交互式、后端导向接口。核心挑战在于：HPC托管的虚拟QPU必须在队列延迟、调度器分配、计算节点隔离和部分执行失败的情况下，保持拓扑、原生门和校准语义，同时不能向集群开放入站路径。

Method: HPC-vQPU采用服务导出架构，将云面向的控制平面与HPC驻留的执行平面分离。控制平面负责设备身份、任务生命周期、快照绑定和事件投影；执行平面通过调度器支持的GPU作业认领并执行工作。协调完全通过出站和代理发起的方式进行。核心抽象是在认领时原子绑定的拓扑和校准感知设备快照，作为不可变契约带入执行，使每个调度作业具有密封性同时保持新设备语义。

Result: 在Pawsey超算研究中心基于Setonix GPU、Qiskit-Aer/cuQuantum和IBM Fez校准数据实现了HPC-vQPU。生产实验表明：服务开销有界且可加，工作负载扩展仅限于模拟器；承载校准的快照产生可测量的输出偏移；认领时绑定防止设备变更后的陈旧执行；并发代理精确完成50/50任务各一次；显式恢复在代理失败后恢复陈旧运行任务。

Conclusion: 研究表明，安全、调度器中介的HPC基础设施能够将设备保真的量子仿真作为交互式虚拟QPU服务导出，成功解决了安全HPC环境与量子软件接口需求之间的矛盾。

Abstract: Device-aware quantum simulation increasingly requires HPC-scale accelerators, yet secure supercomputers expose batch-scheduled execution environments rather than the interactive, backend-oriented interfaces expected by quantum software. The key obstacle is not only remote job submission: an HPC-hosted virtual QPU must preserve topology, native-gate, and calibration semantics across queue delay, scheduler allocation, compute-node isolation, and partial execution-side failures, without opening inbound paths into the cluster.
  We present HPC-vQPU, a service-export architecture for virtual QPUs on batch-scheduled HPC systems. HPC-vQPU separates a cloud-facing control plane, which owns device identity, task lifecycle, snapshot binding, and event projection, from an HPC-resident execution plane, which claims work and realises it through scheduler-backed GPU jobs. Coordination is exclusively outbound and agent initiated. The central abstraction is a topology- and calibration-aware device snapshot bound atomically at claim time and carried into execution as an immutable contract, making each scheduled job hermetic while preserving fresh device semantics.
  We implement HPC-vQPU at the Pawsey Supercomputing Research Centre using Setonix GPUs, Qiskit-Aer/cuQuantum, and IBM Fez calibration data. Production experiments show that service overhead is bounded and additive, while workload scaling remains confined to the simulator; calibration-bearing snapshots produce measurable output shifts; claim-time binding prevents stale execution after pre-claim device mutation; concurrent agents complete 50/50 tasks exactly once; and explicit recovery restores stale running tasks after agent failure. These results show that secure, scheduler-mediated HPC infrastructure can export device-faithful quantum simulation as an interactive virtual-QPU service.

</details>


### [187] [Understanding and Reducing Metadata-Driven Host Overheads in Sampling-Based GNN Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29346)
*Yidong Gong,Saima Afrin,Yuchen Ma,Guannan Wang,Bin Ren,Pradeep Kumar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ZEROGNN通过移除主机端元数据控制循环，实现采样GNN训练的完全GPU驻留执行，获得最高5.28倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习工作负载（尤其是采样GNN训练）呈现动态元数据驱动执行特征，导致CPU处于关键路径，引入持续的主机-设备协调开销和频繁的GPU-CPU同步，在GPU计算量较小时主导端到端运行时间。现有方法（CUDA Graphs和GPU动态并行）因元数据驱动控制循环仍依赖主机中介且执行结构跨迭代变化，无法解决此问题。

Method: 提出ZEROGNN系统，将运行时元数据保留在设备端，在固定启动结构内调解动态执行，并通过提供保守但紧凑的执行包络来恢复CUDA Graph可重放性，从而实现完全GPU驻留执行。

Result: 在采样GNN工作负载上的实验表明，ZEROGNN实现高达5.28倍端到端加速，接近100%的GPU执行占比，内存效率可与理想的元数据感知分配相媲美，并通过消除主机端瓶颈实现强大的多GPU扩展性。

Conclusion: ZEROGNN成功移除了元数据驱动控制循环中的主机依赖，解决了动态执行行为下的GPU驻留执行难题，显著提升了采样GNN训练性能和扩展性。

Abstract: Modern deep learning workloads increasingly exhibit dynamic, metadata-driven execution, where runtime-generated information determines memory provisioning and kernel launch decisions. In sampling-based graph neural network (GNN) training, this behavior places the CPU on the critical path, introducing persistent host-device orchestration overhead and frequent GPU-CPU synchronization, which dominate end-to-end runtime when GPU computation is small. Existing approaches, including CUDA Graphs and GPU dynamic parallelism, fail to address this problem because the metadata-driven control loop remains host-mediated, and execution structure varies across iterations. We present ZEROGNN, a system that removes the host from the metadata-driven control loop and enables fully GPU-resident execution under dynamic behavior. ZEROGNN keeps runtime metadata on-device, mediates dynamic execution within a fixed launch structure, and provisions a conservative yet tight execution envelope to restore CUDA Graph replayability. Experiments on sampling-based GNN workloads show that ZEROGNN achieves up to 5.28 x end-to-end speedup, near 100% GPU execution fraction, and memory efficiency comparable to ideal metadata-informed allocation, while enabling strong multi-GPU scaling by eliminating host-side bottlenecks.

</details>


### [188] [Silent Data Corruption Protection through Efficient Task Replication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29506)
*Mia Reitz,Claudia Fohry*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对大规模超算集群中日益严重的静默数据损坏（SDC）问题，本文提出了一种新颖的复制方案，通过记录任务树并在结果不匹配时自顶向下遍历树来识别和纠正损坏任务，实现了对嵌套fork-join程序的高效SDC检测与恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着超算集群规模不断增大，静默数据损坏（SDC）的发生率也随之上升，可能导致程序结果失效。虽然复制技术是一种常见的SDC保护策略，但将其应用于集群上的异步多任务（AMT）运行时面临挑战，因为动态任务生成和工作窃取机制使得识别被复制任务变得复杂。

Method: 本文提出了一种针对嵌套fork-join程序的复制方案：首先复制计算过程并记录任务树结构；当最终结果出现不匹配时，采用自顶向下的方式遍历任务树，识别所有可能影响最终结果的损坏任务；然后通过重新计算这些损坏任务进行恢复，同时复用正确子任务的结果。该方法在Itoyori集群AMT运行时的变体中实现。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方案能够有效识别并重新处理受影响的任务，且识别和重新处理所需的时间可以忽略不计。此外，论文还讨论了该方案对通过future进行协作的任务的适应性。

Conclusion: 本文成功设计并实现了一种适用于嵌套fork-join程序的SDC检测与纠正方案，在保证计算正确性的同时，将恢复开销降至最低，为大规模超算集群的可靠性提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: The trend of increasing cluster sizes of supercomputers leads to a growing susceptibility to Silent Data Corruption (SDC) that can invalidate program results. A common strategy for SDC protection is replication, where the computation is repeated, and the correct result is determined as the one that is the same in at least two different computations. Applying replication to Asynchronous Many-Task (AMT) runtimes on clusters is challenging due to dynamic task spawning and work stealing, which complicate the identification of replicated tasks.
  To address the challenge, this paper introduces a novel replication scheme that detects and corrects SDCs for nested fork-join programs. Briefly stated, our approach replicates the computation and records the task tree. Upon a mismatch in the final result, it traverses the tree top-down to identify all corrupted tasks that could have impacted the final result. Recovery is then performed by recomputing these tasks, while the results of correct child tasks are reused.
  We demonstrate our implementation within a variant of the Itoyori cluster AMT runtime. Our experimental results suggest that the time to identify and reprocess the affected tasks is negligible. The paper concludes by discussing the adaptability of our scheme to tasks that cooperate through futures.

</details>


### [189] [Design and Implementation of a Serverless MapReduce Framework for Scalable Data Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29573)
*Angelos Dorotheos Chatzopoulos,Babis Andreou,Kakia Panagidi,Stathes Hadjiefthymiades*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于Kubernetes和Knative的事件驱动MapReduce框架，用于物流环境中的实时数据处理，该框架利用Apache Kafka进行组件通信，集成FaaS原则和分布式处理技术，实验证明系统能随数据量增长有效扩展并支持按需处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代物流系统从GPS、物联网传感器和管理系统等源头持续产生大量数据，这些数据的聚合、处理和分析对于监控运营、优化效率和快速响应决策任务至关重要，因此需要一种能够实时处理这些数据的系统。

Method: 提出一个事件驱动的MapReduce框架，运行在Kubernetes与Knative上，使用Apache Kafka作为组件间通信的主干。平台由五个松耦合服务组成，实时接收、处理和聚合数据。使用Redis保存工作流元数据，AWS S3提供持久存储。设计集成了函数即服务(FaaS)原则与分布式处理技术，支持基于工作负载需求和底层硬件的可配置扩展。

Result: 实验评估表明，系统能够随着输入数据量的增加而有效扩展，同时支持按需处理的零扩展（scale-to-zero）能力。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了物流环境中实时数据处理的可扩展性和灵活性，证明了将MapReduce模型与FaaS原则结合在事件驱动架构中的有效性，为物流系统提供了一种高效的实时数据处理解决方案。

Abstract: Modern logistics systems tend to generate continuous streams of data from sources such as GPS, IoT sensors, and logistics management systems. The aggregation, processing, and analysis of data have become vital for monitoring operations, optimizing efficiency, and responding quickly to decision making tasks. In this paper, an event-driven MapReduce framework for real-time data processing in logistics environments is presented. This system runs on Kubernetes with Knative and utilizes Apache Kafka as the backbone for communication between the components. This platform is composed of five loosely coupled services that receive, process, and aggregate the incoming data in real-time. Redis is used to preserve workflow metadata, while an AWS S3 service provides persistent storage for the framework. The design is inspired by the MapReduce programming model. It integrates Function-as-a-Service (FaaS) principles with distributed processing techniques that allow configurable scaling based on the workload demands and the underlying hardware. Experimental evaluation shows that the system can scale effectively as the input data volume increases while supporting scale-to-zero, on-demand processing.

</details>


### [190] [TC-MIS: Maximal Independent Set on Tensor-cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29604)
*Prajjwal Nijhara,Dip Sankar Banerjee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出TC-MIS算法，利用GPU Tensor Core加速最大独立集(MIS)计算，通过将关键计算阶段重构为稀疏矩阵-向量乘法(SpMV)，在多种GPU架构上相比现有方法实现2.84x-18.80x的平均加速比，最高达44.38x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大独立集问题在资源分配、调度和网络优化中具有重要应用。虽然现有GPU算法已能高效处理百万顶点图，但现代GPU配备的Tensor Core具有比CUDA Core高8-16倍的吞吐量，却鲜少用于图算法。研究旨在探索Tensor Core在非结构化图计算中的潜力，以突破传统GPU并行计算的性能瓶颈。

Method: TC-MIS算法将MIS计算的关键阶段重构为稀疏矩阵-向量乘法(SpMV)。具体方法包括：对图邻接矩阵进行分块处理；采用Warp Matrix Multiply-Accumulate (WMMA)操作；将不规则的图遍历转换为规则的大规模并行计算，从而充分利用Tensor Core的高吞吐量特性。

Result: 在支持Tensor Core的多种GPU微架构上进行评估：RTX A5000平均加速2.84x，L40S加速4.84x，H200加速18.80x，RTX 5080加速5.20x。H200上最高加速达44.38x，同时保持与成熟启发式算法相当的解质量。

Conclusion: Tensor Core可有效加速图算法，通过矩阵化重构能将不规则图计算转化为适合Tensor Core的规则并行计算，显著提升MIS问题求解性能，为图算法的GPU优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Maximal Independent Set (MIS) in a graph is a fundamental problem with applications in resource allocation, scheduling, and network optimization. Although graphs are inherently un-structured and challenging for GPU parallelism due to irregular memory access and workload imbalance, specialized GPU algorithms have achieved good performance, processing million-vertex graphs in milliseconds. Modern GPUs are equipped with Tensor Cores (TCs), specialized units for matrix operations with 8-16x higher throughput than CUDA Cores (CCs), which are extensively used for ML, DL, and inference tasks but remain largely unexplored for graph algorithms. In this paper, we present TC-MIS, a TC-accelerated algorithm that reformulates key phases of MIS computation as sparse matrix-vector multiplication (SpMV). TC-MIS tiles the graph adjacency matrix and employs Warp Matrix Multiply-Accumulate (WMMA) operations to transform irregular graph traversal into regular, massively parallel computation. Our evaluation across TC-enabled microarchitectures (Ampere, Ada Lovelace, Hopper, Blackwell) demonstrates that TC-MIS achieves an average speedup of 2.84x on RTX A5000, 4.84x on L40S, 18.80x on H200 GPUs, and 5.20x on RTX 5080 with a maximum speedup of 44.38x on H200 GPU over state-of-the-art methods, while maintaining solution quality comparable to that obtained by established heuristics that produce near-maximum independent sets.

</details>


### [191] [AMDP: Asynchronous Multi-Directional Pipeline Parallelism for Large-Scale Models Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29664)
*Ling Chen,Houming Wu,Wenjie Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出异步多向流水线并行方法(AMDP)，通过限制参数更新次数和启动多个并发流水线，在保持高利用率的同时解决参数不匹配导致的收敛退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异步流水线并行方法在大规模模型训练中存在参数不匹配问题：前向传播和反向传播之间存在参数版本差异，导致收敛性能下降。需要在保持高利用率的同时解决这一收敛退化问题。

Method: AMDP采用三个核心策略：(1)限制每个流水线第一阶段在反向传播前最多处理两个mini-batch，约束前向和反向传播间的参数更新次数；(2)启动多个并发流水线并根据流水线深度自适应调整数量，缓解流水线气泡；(3)跨mini-batch累积梯度并单次更新，确保仅有限数量的mini-batch经历参数不匹配且限制在一个优化步内。

Result: 在GPT和BERT风格模型上的实验表明，AMDP在显著加速训练的同时能够保持收敛性能，有效解决了异步流水线并行中的收敛退化问题。

Conclusion: AMDP成功平衡了训练效率和收敛稳定性，为大规模模型训练提供了一种有效的异步流水线并行解决方案。

Abstract: Pipeline parallelism is essential for large-scale model training, but existing asynchronous approaches often degrade convergence due to parameter mismatch between forward and backward passes. We propose Asynchronous Multi-Directional Pipeline parallelism (AMDP) to mitigate this issue while sustaining high utilization. AMDP limits the first stage of each pipeline to process at most two minibatches before backpropagation, bounding the number of parameter updates between forward and backward passes. To alleviate the resulting pipeline bubbles, AMDP launches multiple concurrent pipelines and adapts their number according to pipeline depth. In addition, AMDP accumulates gradients across minibatches and applies them in a single update, ensuring that only a bounded number of minibatches experience parameter mismatch, limited to within one optimization step. Experiments on GPT- and BERT-style models demonstrate that AMDP significantly accelerates training while preserving convergence.

</details>


### [192] [PRISM: Processing-In-Memory Sparse MTTKRP for Tensor Decomposition Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29728)
*Daniel Pacheco,Leonel Sousa,Aleksandar Ilic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了PRISM，这是首个利用存内计算（PIM）技术加速稀疏张量分解中关键运算spMTTKRP的方法。实验显示，PIM方案和异构协作方案相比最先进的CPU实现分别达到2.37倍和2.64倍加速，且资源利用效率显著高于CPU和GPU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏张量是表示稀疏多维数据的最常用方式，其分解操作在机器学习中广泛应用。CP-ALS是最常用的张量分解算法，其中最耗时的操作spMTTKRP具有强内存受限特性，难以在通用处理器上高效实现。因此需要探索新的硬件架构来解决这一性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出PRISM方法，首次利用存内计算（PIM）技术处理spMTTKRP操作。该方法针对UPMEM PIM架构进行了深入研究，包括：探索不同的分区策略、优化数据格式和核函数优化；提出与CPU的异构协作方案以进一步提升性能。

Result: 实验结果表明：（1）纯PIM方案相比最先进CPU实现最高达到2.37倍加速；（2）异构协作方案最高达到2.64倍加速；（3）虽然UPMEM分布式内存系统在某些工作负载上性能受限，但资源消耗效率（以峰值性能使用比例衡量）显著高于CPU和GPU。

Conclusion: PRISM证明了PIM技术在加速内存受限的稀疏张量运算方面的有效性。尽管存在某些局限性，但该方法在性能加速和资源利用效率方面都展现出明显优势，为稀疏张量计算提供了新的硬件加速思路。

Abstract: Sparse tensors are the most used representation of sparse multidimensional data. Operations that decompose them, selecting their most important features while reducing their dimension, have become prevalent procedures in machine learning. One of the most used tensor decomposition algorithms is the Alternating Least Squares Canonical Polyadic Decomposition (CP-ALS), where the most time-consuming operation is the Sparse Matricized Tensor Times Khatri-Rao Product (spMTTKRP). This operation is strongly memory-bound, making it hard to implement efficiently on general-purpose processors. This work proposes PRISM, the first approach to tackle this operation using Processing-In-Memory (PIM) technology. We extensively characterize different partitioning strategies, number formats, and kernel optimizations that efficiently adapt this operation to UPMEM PIM, which is further boosted by heterogeneous collaboration with the CPU. The experimental results show that the proposed PIM-based and heterogeneous approaches achieve up to 2.37x and 2.64x speedup compared to state-of-the-art CPU implementations, respectively. However, the UPMEM distributed memory system can significantly hinder performance on certain workloads. Nonetheless, the efficiency of resource consumption for this approach, measured by peak performance fraction usage, is significantly higher than for both CPU and GPU.

</details>


### [193] [CARM Tool: Cache-Aware Roofline Model Automatic Benchmarking and Application Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29740)
*José Morgado,Leonel Sousa,Aleksandar Ilic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个自动化的Cache-aware Roofline Model (CARM)框架，支持所有主流CPU架构（Intel、AMD、ARM和RISC-V），实现了跨架构的性能模型构建和应用程序深度分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着HPC系统和CPU架构日益复杂，应用程序开发和优化变得极具挑战性。现有的CARM工具存在明显缺口：Intel Advisor仅限特定厂商，ARM支持不够成熟，而AMD和RISC-V则完全缺乏相应工具。这种工具支持的缺失阻碍了跨架构性能优化的有效实施。

Method: 开发专门定制的汇编微基准测试，覆盖现代CPU从标量到所有支持的向量ISA扩展的完整性能谱系，包括计算单元和所有内存层级。同时，利用性能计数器和动态二进制插桩技术，将应用程序分析集成到CARM框架中。

Result: 实验结果表明，通过该自动化框架构建的CARM屋顶线在各种测试的架构最大值中偏差小于1%，证明了框架的高精度和可靠性。

Conclusion: 该工作成功填补了跨架构CARM工具支持的空白，为所有主流CPU架构提供了自动化的性能模型构建和应用程序分析能力，为HPC应用程序的跨平台优化提供了有效工具。

Abstract: In recent years, HPC systems and CPU architectures as their central components, have become increasingly complex, making application development and optimization quite challenging. In this respect, intuitive performance models like the Cache-aware Roofline Model (CARM) offer effective guidance by providing insights into bottlenecks that limit the application's ability to reach the system's maximum performance. To fully exploit the benefits of CARM optimization guidance for application development, automatic tools for cross-architecture model construction and in-depth application characterization are absolutely essential. Given a plethora of existing CPU architectures, the current landscape of CARM-enabled tools covers either vendor-specific (Intel Advisor), not sufficiently developed (ARM) or simply non-existing (AMD, RISC-V) tools. This is a particular gap that this work intends to close by bringing automatic CARM support to all major CPU architectures and ISAs, i.e., x86 (Intel, AMD), ARM, and RISC-V, by developing assembly microbenchmarks specifically tailored to cover a full performance spectrum of modern CPUs (from scalar to all supported vector ISA extensions) for both computational units and all memory hierarchy levels. Additionally, this work integrates application analysis within the CARM framework using performance counters and dynamic binary instrumentation. Experimental results show that the CARM roofs constructed with the proposed automated framework provide less than a 1% deviation across various tested architectural maximums.

</details>


### [194] [Effective MPI: User-defined Datatypes and Cartesian Communicators for Zero-copy All-to-all Communication in Multidimensional Tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29970)
*Jesper Larsson Träff*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于任意d维环面的零拷贝全全通信算法，通过维度分解和MPI派生数据类型实现高效通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高性能计算中，全全通信（all-to-all）是关键操作，但在多维环面网络拓扑上，标准MPI_Alltoall实现可能无法充分利用网络结构，导致性能不佳。因此需要针对特定拓扑优化通信算法。

Method: 将p个进程分解为d个因子映射到d维环面；使用笛卡尔通信器为每个进程创建d个维度通信器并缓存；将全全通信分解为d次维度上的MPI_Alltoall操作序列；使用MPI派生数据类型实现隐式数据重排，采用双缓冲方案，实现零拷贝。

Result: 实验结果表明该算法与原生MPI_Alltoall实现性能相当，同时揭示了常见MPI_Alltoall实现存在的问题；通过选择p的因子分解和组件MPI_Alltoall操作的实现，可针对特定高性能系统进行算法调优和适配。

Conclusion: 该零拷贝全全通信算法能有效利用多维环面拓扑结构，通过避免显式进程本地数据重排和合理的缓冲策略，实现了与原生实现相当甚至更优的性能，为高性能计算系统提供了可调优的通信解决方案。

Abstract: We present and show how to implement a non-trivial all-to-all communication algorithm for arbitrary $d$-dimensional tori effectively in MPI. Given a factorization of the number of processes $p$ into $d$ factors that can be mapped onto a $d$-dimensional torus, we first utilize a Cartesian communicator to split a given $p$-process MPI communicator into, for each MPI process, $d$ smaller communicators spanning each of the dimensions of the torus to which the process belongs, and cache these communicators in order to avoid expensive splitting at each all-to-all operation. The all-to-all operation itself is decomposed into a sequence of $d$ MPI_Alltoall operations on the dimension-wise communicators. The non-trivial data rearrangement before and after each MPI_Alltoall call is implicit only and effected by MPI derived datatypes. This makes the implementation of the algorithm formally \emph{zero-copy}, meaning that no explicit process-local reordering of data blocks ever has to be performed. In order to achieve this, the algorithm employs a double-buffering scheme with modest temporary buffer requirements. By choosing the factorization of $p$ and selecting appropriate implementations for the component MPI_Alltoall operations, the presented implementation gives ample opportunities for algorithm tuning and adaptation to the particular high-performance system. A few, select experimental results show competitive performance with native MPI_Alltoall implementations and illustrate problems that common MPI_Alltoall implementations may have.

</details>


### [195] [RAFI -- A Ray/Work Forwarding Infrastructure for Data Parallel Multi-Node/Multi-GPU Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30294)
*Ingo Wald,Serkan Demirci,Alper Sahistan,Stefan Zellmann,Andrea Paris,Patrick Moran,Milan Jaros,Tatiana von Landesberger,Ugur Gudukbay,Valerio Pascucci*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RaFI是一个基于CUDA和MPI的软件框架，简化了构建GPU数据并行软件的任务，特别是当光线或类似工作项需要在多个GPU之间迁移时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在构建GPU数据并行软件时，光线或类似工作项常常需要在不同的GPU之间迁移。这一过程涉及复杂的CUDA和MPI编程，现有方法使得开发变得困难且易错，因此需要一个简化的框架来处理这种工作项迁移。

Method: RaFI提供了一个简单的接口，允许CUDA内核直接将工作项转发到其他GPU。在底层，RaFI自动管理所有与CUDA和MPI相关的工作，包括数据传输和通信协调，从而隐藏了复杂性。开发者无需手动处理跨GPU迁移的细节。

Result: RaFI在多个示例应用中展示了其潜力。这些应用证明了该框架能够有效简化GPU间工作项迁移的实现，同时保持良好的性能和可用性。

Conclusion: RaFI成功地为需要跨GPU工作项迁移的数据并行软件提供了一个易于使用的解决方案，显著降低了开发复杂度，并通过示例应用验证了其实用性和有效性。

Abstract: We present RaFI, a CUDA and MPI based software framework that simplifies the task of building GPU-enabled data-parallel software where rays or similar work items need to migrate between different GPUs. RaFI provides a simple interface for CUDA kernels to forward such work items to other GPUs, while under the hood managing all the CUDA and MPI related work required to make this happen. We describe RaFI's motivation and implementation, and show its potential in several example applications.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [Behavior-Induced Mirror-Prox Temporal-Difference Learning for Faster Off-Policy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28849)
*Xingguo Chen,Yuchen Shen,Shangdong Yang,Chao Li,Guang Yang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于行为策略诱导度量的Mirror-Prox时序差分方法(STHTD-MP)，通过利用行为策略转移信息改进鞍点几何结构，相比传统方法具有更优的收敛性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯度时序差分方法的实际性能受辅助变量度量诱导的几何结构强烈影响。传统的Mirror-Prox TD方法通常使用特征协方差度量，而混合TD方法表明行为策略转移信息可以提供更具信息量的更新几何结构。

Method: 提出行为诱导Mirror-Prox时序差分方法(STHTD-MP)，在原始-对偶鞍点公式中用行为策略Bellman矩阵的对称部分替换协方差度量。该方法对原始变量和辅助变量保持单一学习率，并对混合鞍点算子应用Mirror-Prox预测-校正步骤。

Result: 在标准随机逼近假设下证明了固定策略线性预测的收敛性；推导了投影-预言机遍历间隙界和基于确定性Mirror-Prox误差矩阵谱半径的与GTD2-MP的精确均值算子比较。分析表明当行为诱导度量改善鞍点几何时，STHTD-MP比GTD2-MP具有更小的均值收缩因子。在两状态、Random Walk和Boyan Chain基准上的精确数值均值算子分析支持该结论。

Conclusion: STHTD-MP方法通过利用行为策略转移信息改进鞍点几何结构，在满足条件下相比传统GTD2-MP方法具有更优的收敛性能，而Baird反例被识别为严格假设失效的奇异边界情况。

Abstract: Gradient temporal-difference methods provide stable off-policy prediction with linear function approximation, but their practical performance is strongly affected by the geometry induced by the auxiliary-variable metric. Existing Mirror-Prox TD methods typically use the feature covariance metric, whereas hybrid TD methods suggest that behavior-policy transition information can provide a more informative update geometry. This paper proposes a behavior-induced Mirror-Prox temporal-difference method, called STHTD-MP, which replaces the covariance metric in the primal-dual saddle-point formulation with the symmetric part of the behavior-policy Bellman matrix. The method keeps a single learning rate for the primal and auxiliary variables and applies a Mirror-Prox prediction-correction step to the resulting hybrid saddle-point operator. We provide a formal convergence analysis for fixed-policy linear prediction under standard stochastic approximation assumptions: the behavior-induced metric is positive definite, the joint mean system is Hurwitz, boundedness follows from a Lyapunov argument, and the stochastic recursion converges by the ODE method. We further derive projected-oracle ergodic gap bounds and an exact mean-operator comparison with GTD2-MP based on the spectral radius of the deterministic Mirror-Prox error matrix. The analysis shows that STHTD-MP can have a smaller mean contraction factor than GTD2-MP when the behavior-induced metric improves the saddle-point geometry. Exact numerical mean-operator analysis on two-state, Random Walk, and Boyan Chain benchmarks supports this condition, while Baird's counterexample is identified as a singular boundary case where the strict assumptions fail.

</details>


### [197] [Behavior-Aware Auxiliary Corrections for Off-Policy Temporal-Difference Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28855)
*Xingguo Chen,Zhiang He,Yuchen Shen,Shangdong Yang,Chao Li,Guang Yang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出行为感知的BA-TDC和BA-TDRC算法，通过用行为贝尔曼矩阵替代TDC的辅助协方差矩阵来稳定离策略TD学习。实验表明该替换在某些任务中有益，但正则化对于在困难设置中保持鲁棒性能是必要的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在离策略采样下，带函数逼近的时序差分学习可能不稳定。虽然TDC通过辅助协方差修正稳定了离策略TD，TDRC进一步在单时间尺度递归中正则化此修正，但在神经网络值逼近中，特征协方差和时间转移矩阵共同影响最后一层修正动力学，这引发了辅助几何设计问题。本文旨在研究行为感知替代在线性预测设置中的作用。

Method: 首先用行为贝尔曼矩阵(A_μ)替代TDC的辅助矩阵(C)，得到BA-TDC算法；然后对同样的行为感知方程进行正则化得到BA-TDRC。这种两步构造分离了行为感知几何的贡献与正则化的贡献。通过有限状态均值系统形式化，在Hurwitz稳定性条件下证明不动点保持和几乎必然收敛，并通过精确线性误差递归的谱半径比较确定性均值速率。

Result: 在两状态反例、Baird反例、随机游走和Boyan链上的实验表明：行为感知替代本身在某些任务上可以非常有益，但在更困难的设置中，正则化对于获得鲁棒性能是必要的。理论分析提供了辅助几何设计问题的可处理模型。

Conclusion: 行为感知替代可以作为稳定离策略TD学习的有效方法，但需要配合正则化技术才能在各种设置中实现鲁棒性能。这为理解神经网络值逼近中最后一层修正动力学提供了线性分析模型。

Abstract: Temporal-difference learning with function approximation can be unstable under off-policy sampling. TDC stabilizes off-policy TD through an auxiliary covariance correction, and TDRC further regularizes this correction in a single-timescale recursion. This paper studies a behavior-aware replacement of the auxiliary covariance geometry in the linear prediction setting, which is the standard local model for understanding the feature-space dynamics of value-function approximation. We first replace the TDC auxiliary matrix (C) by the behavior Bellman matrix (A_μ), yielding BA-TDC, and then regularize the same behavior-aware equation to obtain BA-TDRC. This two-step construction separates the contribution of behavior-aware geometry from the contribution of regularization. The linear analysis also provides a tractable model for an auxiliary-geometry design question that arises in neural-network value approximation, where feature covariances and temporal transition matrices jointly shape the last-layer correction dynamics. We give a finite-state mean-system formulation, prove fixed-point preservation and almost-sure convergence under a Hurwitz stability condition on the instantiated mean system, and compare deterministic mean rates through the spectral radius of the exact linear error recursion. Experiments on the two-state counterexample, Baird's counterexample, Random Walk, and Boyan Chain show that the behavior-aware replacement can be highly beneficial by itself on some tasks, but that regularization is necessary for robust performance across harder settings.

</details>


### [198] [The Cognitive Categorical Transformer: Category-Theoretic Inductive Biases for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28864)
*Al Kari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Cognitive Categorical Transformer (CCT)，通过引入范畴论中的单纯复形消息传递机制，在参数量相同的情况下将验证困惑度降低了2.92（相对降低12%），首次在3亿参数规模上验证了该机制的有效性，并发现了结构型先验优于一致性先验的经验规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何将认知科学启发与范畴论基础相结合，以增强预训练语言模型的性能。研究旨在验证基于范畴论的组件（特别是单纯复形消息传递）能否在大规模语言模型中带来实质性改进，并区分不同类型的范畴先验对语言建模的影响。

Method: CCT在预训练的GPT-2 Small骨干网络上添加认知驱动的组件。核心方法是GT-Full单纯复形消息传递机制，结合七阶段激活调度。研究采用匹配步数协议（215,000优化器步数、匹配数据和优化器），在WikiText-103上进行严格对比实验，并通过从零重新训练的消融研究来定位各组件的贡献。

Result: CCT在WikiText-103上达到21.27验证困惑度，相比相同调优的GPT-2 Small基线（24.19）降低2.92 PPL（12%相对提升）。消融研究表明GT-Full组件贡献了84%的改进（2.45 PPL）。三项一致性型范畴先验（层平滑、伴随往返、曲率正则化）均未带来改进，支持了"结构/一致性区分"的经验模式。

Conclusion: 首次在大规模语言模型上提供了消融验证证据，证明单纯复形消息传递能显著改善语言模型困惑度。研究发现结构型范畴先验（添加新拓扑）有益，而一致性型范畴先验（强制一致性恒等式）无效，为未来认知架构设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: The Cognitive Categorical Transformer (CCT) is a 306M-parameter architecture that augments a pretrained GPT-2 Small backbone with cognitively grounded components derived from category theory and several inspirations from cognitive science. Under a matched-step protocol (215,000 optimizer steps, matched data, matched optimizer and schedule) on WikiText-103, CCT reaches 21.27 validation perplexity, compared with 24.19 for an identically fine-tuned GPT-2 Small baseline. The architecture therefore contributes a 2.92 PPL (12% relative) reduction beyond what in-domain fine-tuning alone provides. A retrain-from-scratch ablation that holds GT-Full simplicial message passing bypassed across the entire seven-phase activation schedule reaches 23.72 PPL, localizing 84% of the architectural improvement (2.45 of 2.92 PPL) to GT-Full. We present the first ablation-validated evidence that simplicial message passing improves language-model perplexity at the 306M-parameter scale on WikiText-103. Published GPT-2 Large reaches 22.05 zero-shot PPL on WikiText-103 with 6.2x more parameters than GPT-2 Small; this paper treats that number as an external published reference, not as the architectural benchmark. Three negative results on consistency-style categorical priors (sheaf smoothing, adjunction round-trip, curvature regularization) and the joint structural-prior result for GT-Full and PrecisionWeightedPP together support an empirical pattern termed the *structure/consistency distinction*, in which categorical priors that add new topology improve language modeling and those that enforce a consistency identity do not.

</details>


### [199] [Ultra-Reduced-Impact-Encased-Logging (URIEL): propose a new method for selective sustainable logging and post-harvest silvicultural treatment in tropical forest using airborne robotics systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28883)
*Daniel Albiero,Gelton Fernando de Morais,Daniela Han,Flávio Roberto de Freitas Gonçalves,Artur Vitório Andrade Santos,Wesllen Lins de Araújo,Alessandra Maia Freire,Cláudio Kiyoshi Umezu,Mateus Peressin,Francesco Toscano,Admilson Írio Ribeiro,Alfeu J. Sguarezi Filho,Américo Ferraz Dias Neto,Angel Pontin Garcia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合直升机采伐、机器人、AI和无人机后续处理的新型热带森林采伐方法URIEL，实现了高经济可行性并几乎消除了对森林的附带损害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球热带森林面临严重的森林砍伐压力，主要由经济和政治利益驱动，科学证据表明这种砍伐会导致气候变化，因此需要开发一种既能满足木材需求又能保护森林生态系统的采伐方法。

Method: 提出Ultra-Reduced-Impact-Encased-Logging (URIEL)方法，该方法基于直升机采伐技术并结合机器人和AI技术的密集应用，以及无人机执行伐后营林措施。研究开发了适合该方法的设备概念，确定了尺寸参数，完成了数字概念验证，并对多种直升机-木材距离组合进行了数字仿真和经济可行性分析。

Result: URIEL方法具有很高的经济可行性，能够在维持生态系统服务的同时几乎完全消除对森林的附带损害。

Conclusion: 尽管URIEL方法取得了令人满意的科学和技术成果，但其实施可行性取决于与相关利益相关者的整合，包括高科技产业、政治政府、认证伐木公司和本地居民。

Abstract: Tropical forests worldwide are under intense deforestation pressure driven by economic and political interests, and scientific evidence suggests this deforestation contributes to climate change. This paper proposes a novel logging method for tropical forests, Ultra-Reduced-Impact-Encased-Logging (URIEL). This new method is based on heli-logging techniques combined with intensive use of robotics and AI integrated with post-harvest silvicultural treatments performed by drones. The concept of appropriate equipment for this method was developed, dimensions were determined, details were completed in a digital proof of concept, and an effective digital simulation and economic feasibility analysis were carried out for various helicopter-timber-distance combinations. The results demonstrated that a URIEL method has high economic viability and makes it possible to virtually eliminate collateral damage to forests while maintaining ecosystem services. The main conclusion of this paper is that, despite the satisfactory scientific and technological results, the feasibility of a Uriel method depends on the integration of stakeholders intrinsic to the context: high-tech industry; political governments; certified logging companies; and native populations.

</details>


### [200] [Review Arcade: On the Human Alignment and Gameability of LLM Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28897)
*Hans Ole Hatzel,Sebastian Steindl,Jan Strich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了LLM生成的论文评审与人类评审的一致性，发现一致性有限且随提示词和模型变化显著；作者利用LLM迭代修改论文可显著提高评审分数（最多35%的论文），揭示了LLM评审系统的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM生成的论文评审在主要会议中被试点使用，审稿人和作者都可能使用LLM辅助，亟需评估LLM评审的质量以及作者"利用"LLM评审提升分数的可能性。

Method: 基于2025 ACL Rolling Review (ARR)论文进行实证实验：1) 评估LLM评审与人类评审的一致性；2) 测试不同提示词和模型对一致性的影响；3) 模拟作者使用迭代起草-修改工作流，根据LLM评审改进投稿的场景。

Result: LLM评审与人类评审的一致性有限，最佳情况下较为合理，但跨提示词和模型的一致性变化很大。作者利用LLM评审进行迭代修改在特定场景下有效，最多35%的论文获得了统计上显著的总体分数提升。

Conclusion: LLM评审系统存在潜在漏洞，作者可以通过LLM辅助修改来"博弈"评审系统，这为学术出版中使用LLM评审提出了警示和挑战。

Abstract: LLM-generated reviews for scientific papers are gaining considerable traction and are even being officially piloted by major conferences. We have to assume that not only reviewers are using LLM-assistance, but also that authors use LLMs to revise their papers before submitting. In this work, we perform empirical experiments on papers from the 2025 ACL Rolling Review (ARR) to evaluate LLM reviews from both the author and the reviewer perspective. First, we identify a limited alignment of LLM reviews with human ones. In the best-case scenario, the alignment is reasonable. However, we also find that LLM-human alignment varies substantially across prompts and models. Finally, we investigate the scenario in which the author uses an iterative draft-revise workflow to improve the submission according to the LLM review. We find that this "gaming" of LLM reviews can be effective in specific scenarios, leading to a statistically significant increase of overall scores for up to 35\% of papers. We publish our code: https://github.com/uhh-hcds/reviewarcade.

</details>


### [201] [Orthogonal Concept Erasure for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28902)
*Yuhao Sun,Lingyun Yu,Haoxiang Xu,Fengyuan Miao,Zhuoer Xu,Hongtao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出正交概念擦除(OCE)方法，通过乘法参数更新实现扩散模型中概念的精确擦除，在保持模型生成能力的同时可快速擦除多达100个概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现基于编辑的概念擦除方法存在核心局限：依赖加性参数更新，而概念语义主要依赖神经元方向，整体生成能力依赖神经元角度几何，加性更新必然导致概念擦除与生成性能之间的干扰。

Method: 从几何视角将编辑式擦除重构为乘法参数更新；采用闭式解导出的逐层正交变换，保持神经元幅度和角度几何；引入子空间级目标与结构化子空间操作解决多概念擦除中的约束冲突。

Result: 在单概念和多概念擦除实验中均优于现有方法；可在4.3秒内擦除多达100个概念，同时有效保持非目标概念的生成质量。

Conclusion: OCE方法通过正交变换实现了精确概念擦除与生成能力保持的解耦，为扩散模型的安全可控部署提供了高效可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Concept erasure has emerged as a promising approach to mitigate undesired or unsafe content in diffusion models, yet existing methods still face significant limitations. While training-based methods are effective, their high computational cost limits scalability. Editing-based methods are more efficient and deployment-friendly, yet they struggle to simultaneously achieve precise concept erasure and preserve overall generative capacity. We identify this core limitation of the editing-based methods as reliance on additive parameter updates. Our empirical analysis reveals that concept semantics primarily depend on neuron direction rather than neuron magnitude, while overall generative capacity relies on the angular geometry of neurons. As additive updates inherently entangle direction, magnitude, and angular geometry, they inevitably introduce unintended interference between concept erasure and overall generation performance. To address this, we propose Orthogonal Concept Erasure (OCE), which reformulates editing-based erasure as multiplicative parameter updates from a geometric perspective. Specifically, OCE applies layer-wise orthogonal transformations derived from a closed-form solution to the parameters, enabling precise concept erasure while preserving the neuron magnitude and angular geometry. Furthermore, to address conflicting constraints in multi-concept erasure, OCE introduces a subspace-level objective with structured subspace manipulation, yielding a more effective and scalable erasure. Extensive experiments on single- and multi-concept erasure demonstrate that OCE outperforms existing methods in concept erasure and non-target preservation, erasing up to 100 concepts in 4.3 s. Code: https://github.com/HansSunY/OCE.

</details>


### [202] [Frontier LLM-based agents can overcome the ontology curation bottleneck for natural phenotypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28965)
*James P. Balhoff,Hilmar Lapp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了五个前沿大语言模型作为自主策展人进行表型注释的性能，发现所有模型的表现在人类专家的变异范围内，且显著优于之前的NLP工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表型注释（将自由文本描述链接到本体术语）对于比较形态学数据的跨研究整合至关重要，但这一过程依赖高度训练的人类专家，劳动密集且难以扩展，成为关键瓶颈。前期研究表明机器与人类的一致性显著低于人类专家之间的一致性。

Method: 使用来自Anthropic和OpenAI的五个前沿托管LLM作为"自主策展人"，在自包含工作空间中进行评估，提供源出版物PDF、原始人类策展人使用的相同注释指南、四个项目本体（UBERON、PATO、BSPO、GO）和验证脚本。使用Dahdul等人（2018）建立的跨七项系统发育研究的实体-质量（EQ）注释黄金标准进行评估。

Result: 针对相同的黄金标准评估，每个智能体的表现都落在原始研究中三位训练有素的人类生物策展人的策展人间变异范围内；表现最佳的智能体接近但未达到表现最佳的人类策展人水平。智能体在所有四个指标上都显著优于Semantic CharaParser。

Conclusion: 前沿大语言模型能够达到与人类专家相当的表型注释性能，为解决该领域的关键瓶颈提供了可扩展的自动化解决方案，但最佳模型仍未超越顶级人类专家的表现。

Abstract: Linking free-text phenotype descriptions to ontology terms, typically referred to as phenotype annotation, is essential for the cross-study integration of comparative morphological data. This labor intensive process has heavily relied on highly trained human experts, which makes it challenging to scale and thus a key bottleneck. Dahdul et al. (2018) established a Gold Standard (GS) of Entity-Quality (EQ) annotations across seven phylogenetic studies and used it to evaluate three human curators and the Semantic CharaParser NLP tool with ontology-based semantic similarity metrics; they reported that machine-human consistency was significantly lower than inter-curator (human-human) consistency. Here we revisit that benchmark with five frontier hosted LLMs from Anthropic and OpenAI, each operating as an "agentic curator" within a self-contained workspace that supplies the source publication PDF, the same annotation guide used by the original human curators, the four project ontologies (UBERON, PATO, BSPO, GO), and a validation script. Evaluated against the same Gold Standard, every agent fell within the range of inter-curator variability of the three trained human biocurators of the original study; the best performing agents approached but did not reach the best performing human curator. Agents substantially outperformed Semantic CharaParser on all four metrics.

</details>


### [203] [VFEAgent: A Multimodal Agent Framework for End-to-End Automated Finite Element Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28978)
*Jiachen Zhang,Junyi Lao,Chenghao Liu,Siyuan Liu,Shixin Wu,Linsen Zhang,Boyu Wang,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了VFEAgent，一个端到端的多智能体系统，能够直接从输入图像和问题描述自动完成有限元分析（FEA）建模与仿真，显著提高了仿真生成成功率和物理有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限元分析工作流程复杂且高度依赖领域专业知识，现有将大语言模型（LLM）集成到FEA的方法在处理多模态输入和执行复杂任务方面存在局限性，亟需一种能够自动化处理多模态输入并生成可靠仿真结果的解决方案。

Method: 提出VFEAgent系统，包含两个核心组件：（1）多模态视觉-语言多智能体流程，采用ReAct驱动推理从异构输入中提取结构化FEA规格；（2）验证优先的代码合成框架，集成鲁棒的自调试和回退机制以确保可执行性和物理有效性。

Result: 在多种工程力学场景下的系统评估表明，VFEAgent在生成完整且物理有效的仿真方面实现了高成功率，在可靠性和正确性方面优于基于LLM的基线方法。

Conclusion: 研究结果验证了自动化完整FEA工作流程的可行性，展示了该框架将工程师从繁琐手动分析中解放出来的潜力，为工程设计自动化提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Finite Element Analysis (FEA) serves as the cornerstone of modern engineering design. However, its workflow is inherently complex and relies heavily on domain expertise. Although recent efforts have integrated Large Language Models (LLMs) into FEA, existing approaches face limitations in handling multimodal inputs and executing complex tasks. To address these limitations, we propose VFEAgent, an end-to-end multi-agent system designed to automate FEA modeling and simulation directly from input images and problem descriptions. Our methodology integrates two core components: (1) a multimodal vision-language multi-agent pipeline that employs ReAct-driven reasoning to extract structured FEA specifications from heterogeneous inputs and (2) a verification-first code synthesis framework, incorporating robust self-debugging and fallback mechanisms to ensure executability and physical validity. We systematically evaluated the system across various engineering mechanics scenarios. The results demonstrate that VFEAgent achieves a high success rate in generating complete and physically valid simulations, outperforming LLM-based baseline methods in reliability and correctness. These findings validate the feasibility of automating the complete FEA workflow, highlighting the framework's potential to liberate engineers from tedious manual analysis.

</details>


### [204] [BEAMS: Benchmarking and Evaluating AI for Modeling and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28994)
*Sara Metcalf,William Schoenberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BEAMS倡议建立了人机协作的建模与仿真AI工具评估基准，发现AI在定性任务和讨论方面表现优于因果推理和定量纠错，且无单一LLM在所有任务上占优，强调了任务特异性和速度-准确性权衡的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 支持现实决策的AI工具需构建仿真模型以提供可解释的建议，自动化建模工具应补充而非替代人类专业知识，因此需要引导AI建模工具向负责任和符合伦理的方向发展。

Method: 通过开放数字和组织基础设施协作评估AI建模工具；建立sd ai开源项目实现透明度；由指导小组优先排序基准，技术小组实施自动化测试；针对定性建模、定量建模和模型讨论等类别，开发了因果转换、模型迭代、因果推理、一致性、模型行为解释、建议建模步骤和建议模型修复等测试。

Result: 不同LLM与引擎耦合时表现存在差异；AI建模工具在讨论和基础定性任务上表现优于因果推理和定量错误修复；没有单一LLM在所有引擎类型中占主导地位；揭示了特定任务的重要性以及速度与准确性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: BEAMS倡议成功建立了人机协作的建模评估基准，证明了AI工具在不同任务上的能力差异，强调了任务特异性设计和权衡考虑的重要性；未来将纳入解决偏见问题的基准，考虑替代视角和人机协作用例。

Abstract: AI tools to support real world decision making must be able to build simulation models that inform their recommendations and render them interpretable. Tools that can automate aspects of modeling practice must complement human expertise, not replace it. The BEAMS Initiative aims to guide the development of AI tools for modeling and simulation toward forms that are responsible and ethical by establishing benchmarks for human centered modeling and simulation practices. The initiative uses open digital and organizational infrastructure to collaboratively evaluate AI tools for modeling and simulation. The open source sd ai project hosted by the initiative establishes transparency and enables contributions to be shared broadly. A steering group focuses on prioritizing potential benchmarks, while a technical group focuses on implementing the benchmarks in the form of automated tests. Tests for several distinct categories of evaluation have been implemented and applied to AI tools that support qualitative model building, quantitative model building, and model discussion. These include tests for causal translation, model iteration, causal reasoning, conformance, model behavior explanation, suggested model building steps, and suggested model fixes. When engines from the sd ai project are coupled with different LLMs, their performance on these evaluations reveals variability across different AI tools. The evaluations implemented by the initiative demonstrate that AI enabled modeling tools perform better at discussion and basic qualitative tasks than with causal reasoning and quantitative error fixing. No single LLM dominates across engine types, highlighting the importance of specific tasks and tradeoffs between speed and accuracy. Ongoing efforts of the initiative aim to incorporate benchmarks that address concerns about bias by considering alternative perspectives and human centered use cases.

</details>


### [205] [Adopt $\neq$ Adapt: Longitudinal Analyses of LLM Conversations in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29018)
*Rebecca M. M. Hicke,Kiran Tomlinson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过分析约12,000名微软必应Copilot用户的对话轨迹，发现个体用户行为随时间变化极小（高度粘性），而WildChat数据集偏向高水平用户，无法代表典型用户行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于用户与大语言模型（LLM）交互的研究主要呈现静态视角，缺乏对个体用户行为随时间演变的理解，因此需要研究用户行为的动态变化轨迹。

Method: 研究分析了约12,000名随机抽样的微软必应Copilot用户的对话轨迹，并与WildChat-4.8M数据集进行对比，考察群体层面趋势与个体用户轨迹的差异。

Result: 研究发现：1) 虽然群体层面存在显著趋势，但个体用户轨迹的趋势非常微弱，用户习惯具有高度粘性；2) 不同活跃度的用户存在明显差异，活跃用户的对话更成功，且将LLM用于更复杂和专业导向的任务；3) WildChat数据集明显偏向高水平"强力"用户，不能代表典型的用户-AI交互。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明现有用户行为难以改变，揭示了用户异质性的程度，并强调WildChat数据集无法代表典型用户-AI交互，这对该数据的下游应用具有重要提示意义。

Abstract: Although a growing body of research has begun to describe user--LLM interactions, the picture it paints is largely static; little is known about how individual users change their behavior over time. To address this gap, we analyze the conversational trajectories of $\sim$12,000 randomly sampled Microsoft Bing Copilot users and compare these with data from WildChat-4.8M. While the Copilot data contains significant population-level trends, we find that trends in individual user trajectories are much weaker; user habits prove to be overwhelmingly sticky. We also find stark differences between users of different activity levels: more active users have more successful conversations and use the LLM for more complex and professionally oriented tasks. Some user trends also appear in WildChat-4.8M, but we find evidence that this dataset is significantly skewed towards highly proficient "power" users. Ultimately, our results suggest that existing user behavior is difficult to change and demonstrate the extent of user heterogeneity. Our comparison between datasets highlights that WildChat does not represent typical user-AI interactions, an important caveat for downstream uses of the data.

</details>


### [206] [When Models Disagree: Rethinking LLM Evaluation for Public Comment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29025)
*Aisha Najera,Alvin Moon,Vedant Srinivasan,Rajesh Veeraraghavan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了解释性审计管道，将多模型分歧作为解释复杂性的诊断工具，发现LLMs在公共政策评论分类中存在显著的模型间分歧，强调基于分歧的评估是准确率指标的必要补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦机构正部署LLMs对公共评论语料库进行分类，模型的分类结果会影响政策制定者所看到的内容。然而，基于小规模验证集的立场准确率标准评估方法，无法检测不同模型对相同公共输入产生的实质性不同分类，这可能导致重要的公共意见被错误地组织或忽略。

Method: 提出解释性审计管道（Interpretive Audit Pipeline），将多模型分歧作为解释复杂性的诊断指标，引导人工审查真正模糊的公共输入。分析四个LLMs对USDA联邦档案1260条公共评论的分类结果，进行两阶段标注研究：独立标注阶段和查看其他标注后修订阶段，比较四个LLMs和人类标注员的表现。

Result: 模型间主题分歧超过模型内提示变体差异；专家评分标准抑制了深层解释分歧但未真正解决分歧；在修订阶段，不同标注者行为差异显著；人类标注员的修订经常引入了模型集成输出中缺失的框架。

Conclusion: 对于LLM辅助的解释性编码任务，基于分歧的评估是准确率指标的必要补充，能够揭示模型分类中的不确定性和模糊性，为人工审查提供更有效的指导。

Abstract: Federal agencies are deploying large language models (LLMs) to categorize public comment corpora, where the model's organization of the record shapes what policymakers see and which arguments register. Standard evaluation, anchored on stance accuracy against a small validated set, cannot detect when different models produce materially different categorizations of the same public input. We propose an Interpretive Audit Pipeline that treats multi-model disagreement as diagnostic of interpretive complexity and directs human review toward genuinely ambiguous public input. Analyzing 1,260 public comments on a federal USDA docket across four LLMs, we find that inter-model thematic divergence exceeds within-model prompt variation, and that an expert rubric suppresses deep interpretive disagreement without resolving it. In a two-stage labeling study on a stratified 40-comment subsample, four LLMs and a human annotator labeled independently and then revised after seeing the others' labels. Revision behavior varied across labelers, and the human annotator's revisions frequently introduced framings absent from the ensemble's collective output. We argue disagreement-based evaluation is a necessary complement to accuracy metrics for LLM-assisted interpretive coding.

</details>


### [207] [Hallucination Mitigation with Agentic AI, Nested Learning, and AI Sustainability via Semantic Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29055)
*Diego Gosmar,Deborah A. Dahl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于HOPE嵌套学习架构和语义缓存的多智能体幻觉缓解方案，在310个测试提示上实现31.3%-35.9%的幻觉评分降低，同时减少47.3%的LLM调用，证明无需模型重训练即可同时提升事实可靠性、运营效率和可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幻觉仍然是生产级大语言模型系统的主要可靠性障碍，尤其在多智能体流水线中，未经证实的声明可能在不同阶段之间不受控制地传播。现有系统缺乏有效的幻觉检测和缓解机制，需要一种无需模型重训练即可提升事实可靠性的解决方案。

Method: 采用HOPE启发的嵌套学习架构，结合连续记忆系统（CMS）和语义相似性缓存，设计三阶段智能体流水线（通过开放楼层协议OFP编排）。前端智能体配置为高随机性生成器（temperature=1.0）产生真实幻觉基线，第二级和第三级审查器作为渐进式校正器。使用五个KPI指标（FCD、FGR、FDF、ECS、OSR）聚合为总幻觉评分（THS），在五种权重配置下评估缓解-可观测性权衡。

Result: 在五种权重配置下实现端到端THS降低-31.3%至-35.9%。语义缓存在930次潜在调用中实现440次缓存命中（47.3%命中率），将LLM调用减少至490次，降低能耗和CO2e足迹。ExtremeObservability配置获得最负的最终THS（-0.0709），证实重可观测性配置能增强而非妥协缓解效果。

Conclusion: 记忆增强的多智能体设计可以同时提升事实可靠性、运营效率和可审计性，无需模型重训练。可观测性强的配置能够强化而非削弱幻觉缓解效果，使多阶段审查流水线在生产规模上具备运营可行性。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a major reliability barrier for production LLM systems, particularly in multi-agent pipelines where unsupported claims can propagate unchecked across stages. This paper adapts a HOPE-inspired Nested Learning architecture with Continuum Memory Systems (CMS) and semantic similarity caching to a hybrid benchmark of 310 prompts combining 217 epistemic-uncertainty prompts and 93 fabrication-induction stress-test prompts. A three-stage agentic pipeline orchestrated via the Open Floor Protocol (OFP) is evaluated with five KPIs -- FCD (Factual Claim Density), FGR (Factual Grounding References), FDF (Fictional Disclaimer Frequency), ECS (Explicit Contextualization Score), and OSR (Observability Score Ratio) -- aggregated into THS (Total Hallucination Score) across five weighting configurations to study mitigation-observability trade-offs. FDF, ECS, OSR, and FGR are subtracted as mitigation signals, so that a more negative THS indicates stronger mitigation. The FrontEndAgent is configured as a high-stochasticity generator (temperature = 1.0) to produce a realistic hallucination baseline, while the SecondLevelReviewer and ThirdLevelReviewer operate as progressive correctors. This asymmetric design yields end-to-end THS reductions of -31.3% to -35.9% across five weighting configurations. Semantic caching achieves 440 cache hits over 930 potential calls (47.3% hit rate), reducing LLM invocations to 490, lowering energy and CO2e footprint, and making multi-stage review pipelines operationally viable at production scale. ExtremeObservability attains the most negative final THS (-0.0709), confirming that observability-heavy configurations reinforce rather than compromise mitigation. These findings suggest that memory-augmented multi-agent designs can jointly improve factual reliability, operational efficiency, and auditability without model retraining.

</details>


### [208] [Mind Your Tone: Does Tone Alter LLM Performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29027)
*Om Dobariya,Akhil Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究系统分析了提示语调变化对大语言模型在客观多选题任务上准确性的影响，发现调性效应具有系统性和高度模型依赖性，并提出了调性路由框架来解释内部推理模式的调节机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的使用日益普及，但观察到其性能会因提示风格和语调而变化。缺乏对语调变化如何导致模型准确性差异的系统性研究，这对实际部署中的可靠性评估提出了挑战。

Method: 使用两个数据集进行实验：包含5个语调变体的50个基础问题数据集，以及涵盖57个学科、包含7个语调变体的570个MMLU子集。评估了四个高性价比主流模型：ChatGPT-4o、ChatGPT-5-nano、Gemini 2.5 Flash和Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite的性能表现。进一步分析学科级别的语调敏感性差异，并提出路由框架解释语调如何调节内部推理模式。

Result: 跨模型实验表明，语调效应具有系统性但高度依赖模型特性。部分模型呈现小但统计学显著的准确率偏移，而其他模型在不同语调下表现出较大的准确率波动。研究识别出学科级别的语调敏感性差异，发现不同学科对语调变化的敏感程度存在差异。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明用户不应假设大语言模型部署具有语调鲁棒的可靠性。语调变化会系统性地影响模型性能，且影响程度因模型和学科而异，这对实际应用场景中的提示工程和模型选择具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: The use of Large Language Models (LLMs) is proliferating, yet their performance is observed to vary based on prompting styles and tones. In this study, we investigate both whether and how tonal variations in prompts lead to disparate LLM accuracy for objective multiple-choice questions. We use two datasets: a 50-base question dataset with five tone variants and a 570-base question MMLU subset spanning 57 subjects with seven tone variants. Experiments were conducted to evaluate the performance of four cost-efficient, popular LLMs: ChatGPT-4o, ChatGPT-5-nano, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite. Across models, tonal effects are systematic but highly model-dependent. Some models show small, yet statistically significant, shifts, while others exhibit large accuracy swings across tones. Further, we identify subject-level differences in tone sensitivity and present a routing framework to explain how tones may attune internal reasoning modes. Our findings caution users against assuming tone-robust reliability in LLM deployments.

</details>


### [209] [Why Specialist Models Still Matter: A Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Paradigm for Medical Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29744)
*Yanan Wang,Shuaicong Hu,Jian Liu,Guohui Zhou,Aiguo Wang,Cuiwei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HetMedAgent异构医疗多智能体框架，通过协调通用LLM、领域专用模型和临床医生，证明了专家模型的不可替代性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型（如GPT、Claude）在医疗领域的优异表现引发了领域专用医疗模型是否过时的关键问题，需要重新思考医疗AI的发展方向。

Method: 提出HetMedAgent异构医疗多智能体框架，实现冲突感知的证据融合、基于不确定性的临床医生干预触发，以及自适应阈值校准机制。

Result: 在三个真实临床决策任务上的实验表明，通用LLM与领域专用专家模型的协同显著优于单独使用任一类模型，验证了专家模型在模态特定分析中的不可替代价值。

Conclusion: 医疗AI的未来不在于构建单一医疗基础模型或替代人类专家，而在于构建多智能体协作系统，在通用推理能力和领域专用精度之间取得平衡。

Abstract: The impressive performance of generalist large language models (LLMs) such as GPT and Claude in healthcare raises a critical question: will domain-specific medical specialist models become obsolete? We argue that the future of medical artificial intelligence (AI) lies not in building monolithic medical foundation models, nor in replacing human expertise, but in orchestrating collaboration among generalist LLMs, domain-specific specialist models, and clinicians. We propose HetMedAgent, a heterogeneous medical multi-agent framework that enables conflict-aware evidence fusion, uncertainty-based clinician intervention triggering, and adaptive threshold calibration. Experiments on three real-world clinical decision-making tasks demonstrate that the synergy between generalist LLMs and domain-specific specialist models significantly outperforms using either type of model alone, validating the irreplaceable value of specialist models in modality-specific analysis. HetMedAgent represents a shift from building medical LLMs or foundation models to multi-agent collaboration, achieving a balance between general reasoning capabilities and domain-specific precision.

</details>


### [210] [Practitioner Beliefs and Behaviors in AI-Enhanced Education: DOT Framework Survey Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29041)
*David Gibson,M. Elizabeth Azukas,Gerald Knezek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 基于DOT框架对72名高等教育从业者的调查，发现了AI教学整合的信念三因素结构。从业者对AI持积极态度但强调人工监管，实践侧重迭代提示和内容生成，普遍面临政策、培训和基础设施等制度障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在通过DOT框架（整合设计思维和开放系统理论）调查高等教育从业者在AI教学整合中的信念、行为和制度条件，为理解AI在教学中的应用提供实证基础。

Method: 采用横断面调查方法，样本量为72名高等教育从业者。基于DOT框架设计调查，对19个信念条目进行探索性因子分析，考察AI熟悉度、使用模式、设计导向实践和教学信念。

Result: 探索性因子分析识别出三因素结构：AI功能能力、监督与治理、教师协作与规划（α = .90）。从业者对AI作为教学支持持积极看法，同时坚持人工监督和批判性评估；实践强调迭代提示和内容生成，需求评估和反馈循环使用较少；制度障碍广泛存在，包括政策、培训和基础设施不足。

Conclusion: 为DOT框架作为描述从业者信念和实践的模型提供了初步实证支持，揭示了设计导向理论与当前实施之间的差距，贡献了初始测量结构，并为后续验证性研究和结果导向研究指明了方向。

Abstract: This study reports findings from a cross-sectional survey (n = 72) of higher education practitioners examining beliefs, behaviors, and institutional conditions related to artificial intelligence (AI) integration in teaching and learning. Grounded in the DOT Framework, which integrates design thinking and open systems theory, the study investigates AI familiarity, usage patterns, design-oriented practices, and pedagogical beliefs. Exploratory factor analysis of 19 belief items identified a three-factor structure: AI Functional Capabilities, Oversight and Governance, and Instructor Collaboration and Planning (α = .90). Results indicate that practitioners hold favorable views of AI as a pedagogical support while maintaining strong commitments to human oversight and critical evaluation. Reported practices emphasize iterative prompting and content generation, with less consistent use of needs assessment and feedback loops. Institutional barriers including limited policy, training, and infrastructure were widely reported. These findings provide preliminary empirical support for the DOT Framework as a descriptive model of practitioner beliefs and practices, while also highlighting gaps between design-oriented theory and current implementation. The study contributes an initial measurement structure and identifies directions for confirmatory validation and outcome-based research linking AI-supported design practices to instructional quality.

</details>


### [211] [On the Geometry of Games and their Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29919)
*Yaqi Sun,Julian Ma,David Mguni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个学习框架，通过识别游戏的潜在结构属性并自适应地选择或组合求解器原语，来统一理解博弈均衡计算的几何结构，揭示了算法有效性的连续区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的均衡计算研究通常针对特定求解器和特定博弈类别分别进行，导致对求解器行为的理解碎片化。现有的离散分类法无法完整解释算法在何处成功，缺乏对中间或重叠区域的洞察。

Method: 提出了结构感知的求解器合成框架：1）学习一个结构识别器，将每个博弈映射到低维的求解器对齐表示；2）一个策略网络将此表示映射到有效的原语机制；3）引入有界残差作为局部修正器和不完整性的诊断信号。

Result: 实验表明，固定原语表现出系统性的区域不匹配，而学习到的表示将博弈空间组织成与求解器行为对齐的结构化图谱。该框架揭示了特定求解器动力学有效的区域，以及需要混合原语而非单一主导求解器的区域。

Conclusion: 均衡计算应被视为学习求解器机制和映射可解性几何的联合问题。该框架既提供了自适应求解器，也提供了分析视角，展示了具有相似优化动力学的博弈如何聚集在一起，揭示了算法有效性的连续区域和重叠的求解器行为。

Abstract: A central challenge in game theory and learning systems such as GANs is understanding which algorithms can efficiently compute equilibria across the heterogeneous landscape of games. Equilibrium computation is typically studied solver by solver and game class by game class, yielding strong local guarantees but a fragmented view of solver behaviour. Existing discrete taxonomies often provide an incomplete account of where algorithms succeed. We study this problem through a solver-game map linking games to effective solver dynamics. Classical theory identifies isolated regions of this map but provides limited insight into intermediate or overlapping regimes, suggesting that solvability is governed by latent structural properties defining a continuous solver-aligned geometry of games. We formalise this perspective through structure-aware solver synthesis. A learned structure recogniser maps each game to a low-dimensional solver-aligned representation, and a policy maps this representation to effective primitive mechanisms, adapting solver behaviour across regimes. This reveals regions where particular solver dynamics are effective and where mixtures of primitives are required rather than a single dominant solver. A bounded residual acts as a local corrector and diagnostic signal for incomplete solver bases or representations. The framework yields both an adaptive solver and an analytical lens: games with similar optimisation dynamics cluster together, revealing continuous regions of algorithmic validity and overlapping solver behaviour. Empirically, we show that fixed primitives exhibit systematic regime mismatch, while the learned representation organises game space into a structured cartography aligned with solver behaviour. These results suggest viewing equilibrium computation as the joint problem of learning solver mechanisms and mapping the geometry of solvability.

</details>


### [212] [Differentiable Belief-based Opponent Shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29042)
*Aarav G Sane,Karthik Sivachandran,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出D-BOS方法，通过微分k步贝叶斯信念更新来塑造对手信念，在隐藏角色游戏中优于PPO和BBM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体强化学习中的对手塑造方法主要在参数、策略或价值空间操作，而隐藏角色游戏中的信念操纵技术依赖硬编码目标（如欺骗或信念饱和）。需要一种能够自然地从环境奖励结构中涌现最优策略的信念空间塑造方法。

Method: 提出基于可微信念的对手塑造（D-BOS）一阶方法：将观察者的信念视为塑造的对手状态，通过k步softmax-Bayes信念动力学进行微分，将信念状态作为塑造目标而非显式奖励欺骗或合作行为。该方法可自然扩展到多观察者场景，通过聚合各观察者推断信念轨迹的梯度来实现。

Result: 在隐藏角色游戏中，D-BOS性能优于PPO和BBM基线方法，在混合动机设置中优势最为显著。

Conclusion: 信念空间的对手塑造方法能够从环境奖励结构中自然涌现最优策略，为多智能体协作与竞争提供了新的理论框架和实践方法。

Abstract: Human coordination often relies on the ability to influence the beliefs of others through strategic action. In multi-agent reinforcement learning, opponent shaping attempts to replicate this influence, though existing methods typically operate within an opponent's parameter, policy, or value space. Meanwhile, belief-manipulation techniques in hidden-role games often rely on hard-coded objectives, such as deception or belief saturation. We propose Differentiable Belief-based Opponent Shaping (D-BOS), a first-order method that treats each observer's belief as the shaped opponent state and differentiates through $k$-step softmax-Bayes belief dynamics. Rather than explicitly rewarding deceptive or cooperative behavior, our method treats the belief state as the target for shaping. This allows the optimal strategy to emerge naturally from the environment's reward structure. This belief-space formulation provides an opponent-shaping signal by differentiating through opponent belief updates, and naturally extends to multiple observers by aggregating gradients over their individual inferred belief trajectories. Empirically, D-BOS outperforms PPO and BBM in hidden-role games, with the largest gains in mixed-motive settings.

</details>


### [213] [AgentSchool: An LLM-Powered Multi-Agent Simulation for Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30144)
*Yulei Ye,Wenhao Li,Zhong Wen,Yunshu Huang,Yichen Hu,Zifan Wei,Yige Wang,Xinyu Xie,Haoxuan Yang,Yanjun Huang,Ruijia Li,Hong Qian,Yu Song,Bo Jiang,Bingdong Li,Lijun Li,Bo Zhang,Pinlong Cai,Xingcheng Xu,Shuangye Chen,Xia Hu,Liang He,Aimin Zhou,Jingjing Qu,Jing Shao,Xiangfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AgentSchool，一个基于LLM的多智能体教育仿真器，将学习建模为状态转移而非简单的角色扮演，能够生成符合教育理论的学习和社会互动轨迹，为教育AI验证和教育研究提供新工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育AI的验证面临独特困境：干预会影响学习者不可逆的认知和社会发展轨迹，而真实世界的试验缓慢、受伦理约束且受机构限制。现有LLM教育仿真器多将学习简化为角色扮演，仅优化以复现现有课堂，可能在结构上抑制教学改革所需的创新性。

Method: 构建AgentSchool四层架构：(1)可认知成长的学生智能体，配备加权学科知识图谱、思维工作流池和显式错误概念；(2)适应性教师智能体，沿最近发展区(ZPD)进行规划、支架搭建和反思；(3)可配置场景生成器，将教学置于正式与非正式学习场景中；(4)多尺度仿真器，解耦交互规模、时间粒度和仿真时长。核心创新是将学习建模为状态转移而非提示驱动的行为。

Result: 实验表明：结构化学生智能体产生比基线更分化的掌握度和错误概念轨迹；教师智能体呈现与ZPD适应性调整一致的骨干依赖模式；仿真器成功生成符合课堂社会理论的轨迹，包括边缘参与、派系形成、攻击者诱导凝聚和意见领袖涌现等社会现象。

Conclusion: AgentSchool不仅是教育研究工具，更将教育领域构建为社会意义丰富的测试平台，为长时程记忆、多智能体协调和组织压力下的机构推理等AI前沿问题提供了新的实验环境，推动了教育AI验证方法论的发展。

Abstract: Despite the rapid deployment of LLMs into classrooms, validating educational AI remains uniquely intractable: interventions act on developing learners whose cognitive and social trajectories are irreversibly shaped, while real-world trials are slow, ethically constrained, and institutionally locked. LLM-based educational simulators have emerged as a potential remedy, but many still collapse learning into persona-conditioned role-play and, when optimized only to reproduce existing classrooms, can structurally penalize the institutional novelty that pedagogical reform requires. In this work, we introduce AgentSchool, an LLM-driven multi-agent simulator that models learning as state transition rather than prompted behavior. AgentSchool couples cognitively growable student agents -- equipped with weighted subject knowledge graphs, thinking-workflow pools, and explicit misconceptions -- with adaptive teacher agents that plan, scaffold, and reflect along the Zone of Proximal Development, embedded in a configurable scenery generator that situates instruction within both formal and informal learning fields, and a multi-scale simulator that decouples interaction scale, temporal granularity, and simulation duration. Experiments show that structured student agents produce more differentiated mastery and misconception traces than a baseline simulator, while teacher-agent comparisons show backbone-dependent patterns consistent with ZPD-informed adaptation. Further, AgentSchool generates plausible traces of peripheral participation, clique formation, aggressor-induced cohesion, and opinion-leader emergence consistent with classroom social theories. Beyond its role as an educational research instrument, AgentSchool frames education as a socially meaningful testbed for long-horizon memory, multi-agent coordination, and future institutional reasoning under organizational pressure.

</details>


### [214] [Robust and Efficient Guardrails with Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29068)
*Siddharth Sai,Xiaofei Wen,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出COLAGUARD，通过潜在推理将多步安全推理转移到连续潜在空间，在匹配显式推理基线性能的同时实现12.9倍加速和22.4倍token节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于显式推理的安全护栏虽性能优异，但推理延迟和token开销过高，难以满足高吞吐量部署需求；分类式护栏效率高但性能不足。

Method: 提出COLAGUARD，采用阶段式训练策略将多步安全推理蒸馏到连续潜在空间，使推理过程可直接通过隐状态传播完成，无需显式生成推理文本。

Result: 在八个安全基准的十项审核设置上，相对Llama Guard 3提升macro-F1 8.24分；与显式推理基线GuardReasoner性能持平，同时实现12.9倍推理加速和22.4倍token用量降低。

Conclusion: 潜在推理为可部署的安全护栏提供了实用替代方案，能够在提升安全性的同时改善推理效率，两者不再是对立目标。

Abstract: Maintaining the safety of large language models (LLMs) is crucial as they are increasingly deployed in real-world applications. Existing safety guardrails typically rely on single-pass classification or, more recently, distilled reasoning. Reasoning-based guardrails significantly outperform classification-only baselines, but they incur substantial query latency and token overhead that make them impractical for highthroughput deployment. To address this challenge, we propose COLAGUARD, a guardrail model that transfers multi-step safety reasoning into a continuous latent space through a stage-wise training curriculum, enabling direct hidden-state propagation at inference. Evaluated on ten prompt- and response-moderation settings spanning eight safety benchmarks, COLAGUARD improves macro-F1 by 8.24 points over Llama Guard 3 and matches our explicit reasoning baseline, GuardReasoner, in macroF1 while delivering a 12.9X speedup and 22.4X reduction in token usage. Our results suggest that latent reasoning offers a practical alternative to explicit rationale generation for deployable guardrails, jointly improving safety robustness and inference efficiency rather than treating them as competing objectives.

</details>


### [215] [Bridging the Sim-to-Real Gap in Reinforcement Learning-Based Industrial Dispatching through Execution Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29078)
*Jonathan Hoss,Noah Klarmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种策略中立的执行与测量层，用于解决工业环境中事件驱动调度策略在异步和部分观测系统状态下的执行可靠性问题，将不确定的执行失败转化为可归因的结构化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业环境中事件驱动调度策略在异步和部分观测条件下运行，导致决策状态时间不一致、动作可容许性未明确定义、执行错误来源模糊，限制了系统的可靠性和可解释性。

Method: 提出一个策略中立的执行与测量层作为调度策略与工业执行环境之间的中介。该层从异步事件流构建决策有效快照，定义具有明确动作可容许性的标准化执行契约，并记录策略意图、事务结果、物理执行和人工干预之间的偏差，从而实现决策语义与执行行为的分离。

Result: 通过离散事件仿真评估，该框架在所有观测延迟条件下均显示出分析优势，将未分化的执行失败转化为具有完整归因覆盖的结构化类型结果；在低观测延迟条件下操作效益最强，可在提交前避免可预防的执行错误。

Conclusion: 所提出的执行与测量层成功将执行不确定性转化为监督数据，用于评估和策略优化，实现了部署不匹配的可观测性和结构性归因，提升了事件驱动调度策略在工业环境中的可靠性和可解释性。

Abstract: Event-driven scheduling policies are increasingly deployed in industrial environments, where decisions are made under asynchronous and partially observed system states. As a result, decision states are not temporally consistent, action admissibility is not explicitly defined, and the origin of execution errors remains ambiguous. These issues limit both reliability and interpretability.
  To address this gap, a policy-neutral execution and measurement layer is proposed to mediate between scheduling policies and the industrial execution environment. The layer constructs decision-valid snapshots from asynchronous event streams, defines a standardized execution contract with explicit action admissibility, and records outcomes as divergences between policy intent, transactional outcomes, physical execution, and human intervention. This enables a separation between decision semantics and execution behavior and makes deployment mismatch observable and structurally attributable.
  The proposed framework is evaluated using a discrete-event simulation. The results show analytical benefits across all observation lag regimes, as undifferentiated execution failures are transformed into structured, typed outcomes with full attribution coverage. Operational benefits are strongest under low observation lag, where avoidable execution errors can be prevented before commitment. Overall, the layer turns execution uncertainty into supervisory data for evaluation and policy refinement.

</details>


### [216] [The Importance of Out-of-Band Metadata for Safe Autonomous Agents: The Redpanda Agentic Data Plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29082)
*Tyler Akidau,Tyler Rockwood,Johannes Brüderl,Marc Millstone*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Redpanda Agentic Data Plane (ADP)架构，通过带外元数据通道在AI代理读写路径之外强制执行安全治理，解决AI代理作为数字员工时的安全风险问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理越来越多地作为数字员工运行企业数据和自主决策，但其不可预测性（易产生幻觉、误解和对抗性操纵）与技术能力（深度系统知识和高速接口）的结合，使其无法可靠地解释或传播安全关键元数据（如访问策略、数据分类和行为约束），存在安全隐患。

Method: 提出带外元数据通道（out-of-band metadata channels）架构：完全在代理读写路径之外的基础设施通道，携带安全上下文、策略信号和审计跟踪；在代理生命周期的每个阶段强制执行治理：数据输入时限定访问范围、执行期间约束行为、输出时捕获防篡改记录。

Result: 在多代理投资组合再平衡系统中验证ADP：自主代理监控市场、做出交易决策并跨隔离客户账户执行订单；带外通道强制执行每客户数据范围限定、交易审批阈值和防篡改审计跟踪，代理既无法看到也无法绕过这些约束。

Conclusion: 通过将安全治理与代理执行路径解耦，ADP架构实现了对AI代理的可靠安全管控，确保即使代理存在幻觉或被操纵，也能强制执行访问策略和数据保护，为AI代理安全运行提供了可行的基础设施解决方案。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly expected to operate as digital employees: accessing enterprise data, making decisions, and taking actions autonomously. But agents are simultaneously less predictable than humans -- prone to hallucination, misinterpretation, and adversarial manipulation -- and more technically capable: with deep system knowledge and high-throughput interfaces cascading damage at machine speed. This combination makes it unsafe to rely on agents to faithfully interpret or propagate security-critical metadata such as access policies, data classifications, and behavioral constraints.
  We present the Redpanda Agentic Data Plane (ADP), an architecture built around out-of-band metadata channels: infrastructure pathways that carry security context, policy signals, and audit trails deterministically, entirely outside the agent's read and write path and across heterogeneous infrastructure. These channels enforce governance at every stage of the agent lifecycle -- scoping data access on the way in, constraining actions during execution, and capturing tamper-proof transcripts on the way out.
  We demonstrate ADP with a multi-agent portfolio rebalancing system in which autonomous agents monitor markets, make trade decisions, and execute orders across isolated client accounts -- with per-client data scoping, trade approval thresholds, and tamper-proof audit trails all enforced by out-of-band channels the agents can neither see nor bypass.

</details>


### [217] [The Chain Holds, the Answer Folds: Trace-Answer Dissociation in Reasoning Models Under Adversarial Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29087)
*Yubo Li,Ramayya Krishnan,Rema Padman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了推理模型在多轮对抗对话中的“不忠实屈服”(UC)现象：思维链保持正确但最终答案翻转错误。推理模式下的潜在正确率约50%，无推理模式下降至11-15%，证明了推理过程与输出之间的忠实性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理模型仅在单轮基准测试中评估，但实际部署于多轮对话场景，用户会对正确答案提出质疑。研究发现现有评估方法（翻转率指标和单轮忠实性探针）无法检测到思维链正确但答案错误这一隐蔽失效模式。

Method: 提出$2\times 2$潜在-行为框架隔离UC现象，在三个数据集（MT-Consistency、MMLU-Pro、GSM8K）上测试，对比think模式和no_think模式。使用GPT-4o作为独立评判者验证UC标签，并采用token级探针分析答案槽位。

Result: think模式下潜在正确率在行为翻转点聚集于50%附近，no_think模式下崩溃至11-15%，提供了配对、模型内因果证据。效应与推理通道相关：Qwen3-32B和GPT-OSS-20B中高，inline-CoT Gemma-4-31B-it中低。GPT-4o评判者验证86%的UC标签，token级探针显示84%的UC单元中答案槽位argmax正确。

Conclusion: 推理模型存在此前未记录的失效模式——不忠实屈服，思维链保持事实正确但答案翻转错误。推理过程本身造成了忠实性差距，简单的trace锚定防御会产生反效果。研究者发布了所有轨迹、追踪和评判标签以供后续研究。

Abstract: Reasoning models are evaluated on single-turn benchmarks but deployed in multi-turn dialogue, where users push back on correct answers.
